| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 1 | Prepare a unique AR PASSE inbound 834 file for one member with NM109 = [TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file], populate Loop 2000 DTP03 with [TEST DATA: effective date in CCYYMMDD format], do not populate Loop 2300 HD03, keep the maintenance date segment out of the date-selection input, and save the file with a unique control number and file name. | The test file contains one member transaction, the member SSN is present in NM109, only the Loop 2000 DTP03 date is present among the three candidate dates, and the file name and control number are unique. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 2 | Place the prepared file in the NAS inbound staging folder \\daycrtappfs01\EdifecsSTRoot\834\Inbound. | The file is visible in \\daycrtappfs01\EdifecsSTRoot\834\Inbound before pickup. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 3 | Monitor the staging folder until Edifecs automatically picks up the file from \\daycrtappfs01\EdifecsSTRoot\834\Inbound. | The file is removed from the staging folder by the automated inbound pickup process. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |

| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 7 | Open the AR PASSE 834 transaction and review the processing status and delivery status for the file. | The transmission shows successful processing and indicates policy unit delivery completed successfully. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |

| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 9 | Run the archive validation query on crt_72.sql.caresource.corp\crt_72: SELECT * FROM EDIStageArchive.dbo.Interchange WITH (NOLOCK) WHERE ControlNumber = '[TEST DATA: ISA13 or interchange control number from the mocked 834 file]'; | Exactly one Interchange row is returned for the submitted control number. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 10 | Run the transaction archive validation query on crt_72.sql.caresource.corp\crt_72: SELECT TS.* FROM EDIStageArchive.dbo.TransactionSet TS WITH (NOLOCK) JOIN EDIStageArchive.dbo.Interchange I WITH (NOLOCK) ON TS.InterchangeID = I.InterchangeID WHERE I.ControlNumber = '[TEST DATA: ISA13 or interchange control number from the mocked 834 file]'; | At least one TransactionSet row is returned for the submitted interchange and the 834 transaction is recorded in the archive database. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 11 | Connect in SQL Server Management Studio to Database Engine crt_69.sql.caresource.corp\crt_69 using Windows Authentication for Facets validation. | A successful SQL connection is established to crt_69.sql.caresource.corp\crt_69. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 12 | Run the SSN-based Facets eligibility validation query on crt_69.sql.caresource.corp\crt_69: SELECT SBEL.* FROM FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_SBEL_ELIG_ENT SBEL WITH (NOLOCK) JOIN FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_MEME_MEMBER MEME WITH (NOLOCK) ON SBEL.SBSB_CK = MEME.SBSB_CK WHERE MEME.MEME_SSN = '[TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file]'; | At least one CMC_SBEL_ELIG_ENT row is returned for the submitted member SSN. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 13 | Run the member and subscriber validation query on crt_69.sql.caresource.corp\crt_69: SELECT MEME.MEME_CK, MEME.SBSB_CK, MEME.MEME_SSN, SBSB.SBSB_CK FROM FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_MEME_MEMBER MEME WITH (NOLOCK) JOIN FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_SBSB_SUBSC SBSB WITH (NOLOCK) ON MEME.SBSB_CK = SBSB.SBSB_CK WHERE MEME.MEME_SSN = '[TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file]'; | One member row and its linked subscriber row are returned, confirming the member and subscriber records were loaded. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
| TC_001 | Verify AR PASSE inbound 834 uses Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date as the transaction effective date when only that date is supplied | Validate that an AR PASSE inbound 834 enrollment containing only the Loop 2000 DTP03 effective date is successfully processed and the Facets transaction effective date is loaded from that single date value. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one member in CRT with a unique inbound 834 file prepared for trading partner AR PASSE, the file contains the member SSN in NM109, contains Loop 2000 DTP03 only as the transaction-driving date, omits the Loop 2300 HD03 assessment date and omits the Loop 2000 maintenance date indicator intended for transaction date selection, and the member is eligible to be loaded through normal inbound enrollment processing. | 14 | Run the effective-date verification query on crt_69.sql.caresource.corp\crt_69: SELECT MEME.MEME_SSN, SBEL.SBEL_EFF_DT, SBEL.SBEL_ELIG_TYPE, SBEL.SBEL_VOID_IND FROM FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_SBEL_ELIG_ENT SBEL WITH (NOLOCK) JOIN FACPRDDB.dbo.CMC_MEME_MEMBER MEME WITH (NOLOCK) ON SBEL.SBSB_CK = MEME.SBSB_CK WHERE MEME.MEME_SSN = '[TEST DATA: member SSN used in the mocked 834 file]' ORDER BY SBEL.SBEL_EFF_DT; | The loaded eligibility row includes SBEL_EFF_DT equal to [TEST DATA: effective date in CCYYMMDD format], confirming the transaction effective date was populated from Loop 2000 DTP03. | Manual | New | P1 |  | EDI |  | Functional |  |
